1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/nI lAm+ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni yak+Sha ma hA rU dra badzra a na la dzi hwa 
1b2(rgyud): tan+t+ra nA ma/bod skad du/phyag na rdo rje gos sngon po can gnod sbyin drag po chen po rdo 
1b3(rgyud): rje me lce'i rgyud ces bya ba/'phags pa phyag na rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag 
1b4(rgyud): gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das thams cad 'byung ba'i yum btsun 
1b5(rgyud): mo'i b+ha ga la bzhugs te/de nas gnod sbyin drag po bye ba phrag bcu dang /stong gsum 
2a1(rgyud): @#//gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na drag cing gtum pa'i rigs ji snyed pa thams cad dang thabs gcig 
2a2(rgyud): go/de nas bcom ldan 'das drag po'i skyil mo krung bcas nas g.yas pa nam mkha' la bsgreng /g.yon pa sa la 
2a3(rgyud): bgrad nas spyan bzlog pas phyogs bcur gzigs te/zhal nas hUM ha zhes pa'i sgra cher byung ngo//de nas 'khor thams cad 
2a4(rgyud): gtso bo mi snang bas yun ring du brgyal bar gyur to//de'i skad cig de nyid la bcom ldan 'das kyi spyi bo'i gtsug nas sangs rgyas 
2a5(rgyud): thams cad kyis gnang ba/sngags rnams pa gsum gyi bdag por gyur pa/phyag brnyan lag na rdo rje zhes bya ba kha dog sngon po 
2a6(rgyud): cher grags pa/phyag g.yas pa na rdo rje rtse dgu pa thogs pa/g.yon pa na nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po drag po thogs pa zhig 
2b1(rgyud): rgya mtsho chen por zhugs so//de nas nam mkha' lding gi gshog   pa bskyod pas rgya mtshor zhugs te/klu'i rgyal pa dga' bo dang /'jog pos rang gi bdag po'i spyi bor 'dud 
2b2(rgyud): do||nye dga' ba dang dung skyong gis rna cha byas so//rigs ldan gyis mgul rgyan du byas so//pad+ma dang pad+ma chen pos lag gdub tu byas so//sprin gyi shugs can gyis se ral khar 
2b3(rgyud): bcings so//hu lu hu lus ska rags su byas so//'brug sgra dang bdud 'joms kyis   rkang gdub tu byas te/slar bdag po'i lag pa g.yon par song ngo //de nas gnod sbyin bye ba 
2b4(rgyud): phrag bcu dang /drag po'i rig+sa rnams brgyal ba l+sa sangs te/phyogs kun tu bltas pa las phyag brnyan lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho'i nang na 'dug pa mthong nas/rings par kye ma a la la   
2b5(rgyud): bdag cag gi gtso bo nyid bzhugs so//zhas brjod nas song ba las phyin te/bltas pas cher drag pa skra kham pa gyen du 'phags pa/mig me ma dag gi phung po ltar 'phar pa/
2b6(rgyud): mche ba gtsigs pa/lce glog ltar 'gyu ba/gsus pa rdzing ltar 'khyil pa/rgya mtsho'i zab su dpag tshad brgyad khri yod pa rkang pa'i spus mor phyin pa/klu'i rgyan cha bdun dang ldan 
2b7(rgyud): pa|lag pa g.yas na rdo rje rtse dgu pa thogs pa/g.yon na nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po drag po thogs pa/sku byin po la dpag tshad nyis 'bum drug khri yod pa bzhugs so//
2b8(rgyud): de nas gnod sbyin rnams kyis gsol pa/gtso bo khyod kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba'i don drag po rgyas par gson cig dang bdag cag rnams kyis zhu bar ma spobs 
3a1(rgyud): @#//gyis zhes gsol nas/lag na rdo rje la gos sngon po dang la bai DUr+ya zhun ma'i nang du btsos bzhig bla gos su gsol te/stag gi dpags pa drug stong gis sham thabs 
3a2(rgyud): su bcings so//de nas lag na rdo rje rings par bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar song ste zhabs la spyi bos gtugs te/mdun du snying po dang ldan pa'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas te 'dug go/
3a3(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis nam mkha' dang sa gzhil gzigs nas/bar snang du yang spyan gyis 'khor bar gzigs so//de nas khaM bruM saM/zhes bya ba'i sngags brjod do//de nas 
3a4(rgyud): lag na rdo rje stan las langs nas/rdo rje thal mo rang gi snying gar sbyar te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba drug po'i don bdag dang 
3a5(rgyud): sems can thams cad la phan pa'i phyir zhun/ci bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs dbye bar gnang ngam zhes gsol pa dang /ci yang mi gsung bar bcom ldan 'das gdug pa'i 
3a6(rgyud): tshogs nges par 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/zhugs ma thag tu stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na 'khod pa'i gdug pa can thams 
3a7(rgyud): cad snying 'dar bar gyur to//de nas ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs nas/lag na rdo rje la gzigs te byed pa g.yon pa'i chu shing snying po nas drangs te bkug pa'o//byed pa skyes pa'i chu 
3a8(rgyud): shing sgra dang bcas pas brkyang ba byas te gnas so//de nas lag na rdo rje phyag rgya de nyid yang dag par rig ste/tshig 'di skad ces brjod de//khyed la sogs pa'i 'khor dgas phyogs 
3b1(rgyud): 'dir ma gnas par phyogs gzhan du dengs shig/gal te mi 'gro na khyed la sogs pa'i snying 'gas par 'gyur ro//zhes brjod pas sgra gnas kyi khung bur sgrib byed kyi tshogs song ste/snga ma 
3b2(rgyud): bzhin du 'khod do//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o//!//de nas phyag na rdo rje yis//thub pa'i sku la 'di skad gsol//gsang ba'i rdo rje drag po'i 
3b3(rgyud): don||rgyas par bdag la bshad du gsol//gsang ba'i bdag po legs par yang //nyon la yid la zung shig khyod//nga yi gsang ba gang yin dang //rdo rje drag po gang yin pa//nga la 
3b4(rgyud): mtshan nyid gzhan med de//khyod nyid kyi ni rgyu dang yang //khyod kyi bya ba'i las bshad bya//skye ba bzhi bsdus sems can rnams//gdug pa can gyis gtses gyur pa//thabs su ldan pa'i bzlog 
3b5(rgyud): pa yi||mtshan ma nges par bshad par bya//ba ni rnam par snang mdzad de//dza ni mi bskyod thub pa'i sku//ra ni rin chen 'byung ldan sku//me lce yig 'bru gsum po la//dang por gtso bo bshad bya 
3b6(rgyud): ba||pA ni rdo rje 'joms byed ma//Ne ni padam 'joms byed ma//i ni kun 'gro 'joms byed ma//'og btags gsum po ming mthar sbyar//e ni kun tu go byed do//da ni smin thar byed pa ste//
3b7(rgyud): ba ni kun nas bsdud pa yin//sa ni yongs su rten pa'i las//ya ni yang dag bskyod par bshad//go 'byed gnyis pa'i steng gnas pa'i//sna lan dag tu shes par bya//de las nges par 
3b8(rgyud): 'byung bas na//kun snang gang yin dngos dang sbyar//bsam pa'i dngos la 'jug rnams kyi//yan lag thabs rnams thams cad du//gong du smos nas brjod pa yin//don du gsal bar zlos 
4a1(rgyud): @#//ba yi//tshig ni rgyas par brjod mi dgos//bsams grub pa'i gzugs rnams la//drag po yongs su ston pa dang //gnod sbyin be con thogs rnams la//gtso bo'i don 
4a2(rgyud): du bshad pas na//tshig tu nges par yang dag brjod//khyod kyi lung 'chang rab dga' bas//de nyid rigs su byung ba ste//kun kyang drag po'i bsal dga'//snang ba dag par mi mthong bzhin 
4a3(rgyud): de nyid la ni nges par brjod//gong du bstan pa gang yin pa//so so dag la gdon mi za//sbyor ba sna tshogs nges bsgrubs na//snang bar 'byor pa sna tshogs pa//mngon du rgyas shing 
4a4(rgyud): nges par gsal//snang ba chen po bdag med par//rgyud pas yongs su grub pa yin//kun rdzob gong ma'i tshig lta bu//dngos po bsten pa lta bur mthong //don dam 'og ma'i sgra lta bur//
4a5(rgyud): nyon mongs kun bsregs rang bzhin gyis//'od gsal ba ru gnas pa min//snying po don dam la 'jug pa//phyi rim kun rdzob la brten bya//dang por sangs rgyas mi gnas shing //snga ma'i 
4a6(rgyud): bya ba sbyor bar brtson//dbang thob pa la rlom sems med//bla ma'i man ngag nges pa las//bla ma nyid la ci nus kyis//yang dag par ni mchod par bya//rdo rje ngo bo kun 'byung dang //gdug 
4a7(rgyud): pa thams cad sreg phyir gang //dngos po la 'jug gong ma'i sgra//gong ma'i tshig tu brjod pa yin//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can//rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las/nges tshig gi le'u ste gnyis pa'o//
4a8(rgyud): gos sngon lag na rdo rje yis//sangs rgyas mgon la 'di skad gsol//gang zhig bsgrub par bya ba yin//thabs kyi dkyil 'khor gsung du gsol//rig pa'i rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//
4b1(rgyud): yang dag don dam dngos gyur na//dkyil 'khor lha gzugs med pa ste//'gro ba rtog pa la gnas pas//de yi kun rtog gsal ba'i phyir//lha yi sbyor ba bshad par bya//gang zhig mos par 
4b2(rgyud): byed pa yis//khru gsum dkyil 'khor phyogs bzhi pa//nags sam dur khrod khang stong ngam//gtsug lag khang ngam mchod rten nam//de dag gnas su nges par brtsam//dang por bsten 
4b3(rgyud): pa rab gnas pas//bu mo bcu drug ston mo byas//bdag gis zhag gsum khrus bya zhing //spyi bo lkog ma snying kha ru//gsang ba gsum gyi yi ge brtag/rams su bcos pa'i gos nyid la//
4b4(rgyud): gur kum thig le nges btab ste//de nyid la ni bskon par bya//zas su dkar gsum sbyin byas shing //skyes pa dang ni phrad mi bya//snying po 'di nyid 'bum tshad du//rang gis bzlas pas rig pa 
4b5(rgyud): 'byin//de nas bsnyen pa rdzogs pa las//bu mo nyid la tshig brjod de//yi ge rnam lnga nyid dang yang //rams dang mthing shun kun 'dus su//rin thang mi brtsi nyor bcug ste//de nyid rigs mthun 
4b6(rgyud): nang btums la//gnas gang yin par bdag la btang //de yis zhag gsum sbyor ba yis//phyag rgya nges pa nyid dang yang //snying po gong gi sbyor ba yis//dri chu btab la grangs tshad du//phyin par 
4b7(rgyud): yang dag bzlas par bya//de nas skud pa sngon po ni//bu mos legs par bkal ba la//sngags kyis dag byas rab mdzes pas//srad bu dgu ru nges par sgrel//me tog sngon po'i nang btums 
4b8(rgyud): la//seng ldeng phur pa lnga rnams kyis//srad bu yongs su dri bar bya//rab tu tshad du nges pa yis//phyag rgya ma la dag brjod pas//de nyid mnyam par bzhag pa'i//de nyid 'khor legs par byas pa 
5a1(rgyud): @#//yin||'phangs su khru dang tshad byas te//dang po byang shar mtshams nyid nas//kun rtan kha dog rigs mthun par//yongs su bsdus byas nges par bya//gnyis pa gnyis pa nyid du nges//gsum pa gcig gi tshad dang 
5a2(rgyud): ldan||de ltar chod du ldan pa las//shin tu mnyam par 'thas par bya//de nyid rjes su 'jug pa yis//dang po'i srad bu gang yin pas//kha dog ser po'i thig gdab bya//dkyil 'khor dgu ru nges//
5a3(rgyud): pa la||'bru dangs dang rin chen dang //phye ma nyid du btags pa yis//dkyil du pad+ma bcu drug pa//'dab mar ldan par bri bar bya//shar dang lho dang nub byang dang //mtshams rnams su yang de bzhin 
5a4(rgyud): bri||yi tshe'i pad+ma gang yin pa//sna tshogs par ci yang dag bshad//mtshams rnams su ni mchod pa yi//lha mo yongs su dgod par bya//sgo bzhi dag du khro mo bzhi//sbyor bas yang dag dgod par bya//
5a5(rgyud): rin chen pha gu'i dod yon dang //dra ba dra phyed yang dag ldan//twa ra Na ni bzhis brgyan cing //sna tshogs rdo rje gzhir gnas yin//gdugs dang ri dgas gang yin pa//rgyan rnams kun gyis 
5a6(rgyud): rnam par sgeg/sngags pa byang chub blo ldan pas//keng rus phreng ba nyid dang yang //sbrul mgo'i phreng ba'i rnam par rgyan//rdo rje sna tshogs rab 'bar ba'i//phreng ba'i phyi rol legs par bskor//
5a7(rgyud): zur bzhi dag tu bum pa bzhi//rgyan gyis rdzogs par yang dag bri//de dkyil phyag na rdo rje nyid//snga ma'i tshul gyis yongs su gnas//shar du khams gsum rnam rgyal nyid//yang dag par 
5a8(rgyud): ni bre bya ste//kha dog dmar po nyid la yang //dbu gsum phyag ni drug pa'i dbyibs//zhabs bzhi brkyang bskum tshul du bzhugs//pho ni rdo rje nges pa las//mo ni dgra sta yang dag ldan//
5b1(rgyud): pa dam gnyis kyis 'ching byed pa//go dam mthe bo des mdud nas//gzhan rnams yongs su brkyang ba yin//de nyid snying po gang yin pa//snying gar yang dag gzung byas nas//de 'og pho yes zhags pa 
5b2(rgyud): 'dzin||mo yis ral gri bzung byas pa//dang po nyid la dgod par bya//lho ru nag po chen po nyid//kha dog dud sprin lta bu la//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyis pa'i dbyibs//pho nyid tsan dan bde chen 
5b3(rgyud): bsnams//mo ni zhags pa yongs su 'dzin//khro zhal gzigs pa mi zad pas//phyogs bcu rnams su rab tu gzigs//de nyid mgon po gang yin pa//phyogs gnyis par ni rab tu dgod//
5b4(rgyud): nub phyogs gsum par dgod bya ba//lus ngan sras po stobs po che//kha dog ljang gu nyid la yang //chung zad spyan ni rgyas pa nyid//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyi sa pa la//spyi bor rdo rje rmog gis brgyan//g.yas 
5b5(rgyud): pa nyid la rdo rje can//g.yon pa ne'u le'i rkyal pa 'dzin//zhabs gnyis drang por rab gnas te//shar phyogs bltas pas yongs su gnas//byang phyogs bzhi par dgod bya ba//bdud rtsi 'khyil ba 
5b6(rgyud): yongs su gnas//kha dog sngon po de nyid la//tsham rngam khro gnyer brjid pa'i zhal//g.yas bskum g.yon brgyang stangs stabs tshul//phyag gnyis brang du rab bsnol te//g.yon du nges par cung zad 
5b7(rgyud): gzigs//lhor bltas yang dag dgod par bya//byang shar rdo rje gnod sbyin te//kha dog dmar nag 'jigs pa la//g.yas brkyang g.yon bskum rab bzhugs nas//phyag g.yas gri gug rnon po 
5b8(rgyud): 'phyar||g.yon pa cang te'u drag po rdung //gtso bo la bltas dgod par bya//shar lho rdo rje ro langs nyid//kha dog sngo skya rab 'bar la//mi bzad 'bar ba 'jigs pa'i gzugs//cung zad g.yas su 
6a1(rgyud): @#//nges par gzigs//g.yas brkyang g.yon bskum tshul gnas te//rdo rje lcags sgrog rab bzung nas//gung srin la sogs gsum po rnams//dam bor bkug bas 
6a2(rgyud): yongs su bsdams//mtheb 'dzub bzhi rnams rab brkyang ste//spyi bor nges par bsnol bar bsnams//lho nub rdo rje mkha' 'gro ste//kha dog dmar skya nyid la yang //shin tu mi bzad 
6a3(rgyud): 'jigs pa'i gzugs//zhabs gnyis mthil sbyar btsugs pa la//nam mkha' gru bzhi ba ru 'dod//yag gnyis spyi bo thal sbyar nas//me lce 'bar ba yongs su 'dzin//thod pa lnga yis 
6a4(rgyud): mgo brgyan la//gtso bo la bltas dgod par bya//nub byang bzhi pa'i mtshams su yang //rdo rje 'jigs byed zhes bya ba//kha dog sngo nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//zhal gsum phyag ni 
6a5(rgyud): drug pa la//zhabs gnyis dag du rnam par bshad//g.yas g.yon dang po glang chen gyi//pags pa yongs su brkyang pa 'dzin//de 'og g.yas pa dgra sta la//g.yon pa rdo rje yang dag 
6a6(rgyud): 'dzin||gsum pa'i g.yas na lcags kyu ste//g.yon na mi mgo rlon pa bsnams//brkyang bskum tshul tu gnas nas kyang //gtso bo nyid la kha bltas bzhugs//mtshams kyi gru tshad 
6a7(rgyud): gang yin par//'od skyed ma dang yid 'phrog ma//kun bzang ma dang glu sgra can//rang gi phyogs kyi kha dog la//shin tu zhi bas ldem bag can//phyag g.yas su ni me tog dang //dri dang 
6a8(rgyud): zhal zas sgra brnyan rnams//gar gyis rol bar yang dag ldan//nu ma cung zad 'bur ba la//dar dag dkar pos smad g.yogs pas//gtso bo la bltas mdzes pa'i gzugs//phyi yi 
6b1(rgyud): grwa ru dgod par bya//sgo rnams su ni sgo skyong ni//srog gcod ma dang dbugs rdub ma//khrag mthung ma dang sha za ma//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyis pa la//stag dang seng ge sbrul dang yang //dom du yang 
6b2(rgyud): dag gnas pa ste//phyag g.yas tho ba rgyal ba dang //hom gzar gri gug rnon po bsnams//g.yon pa rnams ni zhags pa nyid//kun kyang 'jigs par yongs su 'dzin//rang gi phyogs 
6b3(rgyud): kyi kha dog ste//kun kyang nag po shin tu bsreg/g.yon brkyang g.yas nas bskums pa nyid//mche ba btsigs shing rngam pa'i sku//shin tu khro gnyer brjid pa'i zhal//gar stabs kyis rol 
6b4(rgyud): bde chen gnas//cung zad g.yon du gzigs pa yis//ha la ha la'i sgra yang sgrogs//gzhan yang lha rnams de dag kun//thod pa lnga yis brgyan byas nas//rgyan cha lnga dang yang dag ldan//kun kyang stag gi 
6b5(rgyud): pags pa yis//smad ni yongs su dkris pa yin//de stengs gdan ni rab gnas pa//gtso bo nyid la nyi zla yis//phrag pa nyid la 'khor bar rgyu//tshangs pa dbang po lha chen gnyis//zhabs kyis yongs 
6b6(rgyud): su mnan pa yin//shar gyi lha chen rnam rgyal nyid//keng rus steng na bzhugs pa bzhin//gzhan yang de ltar gnas pa yin//sa bon gnas pa gang yin pa//sgra dang bcas pa shes par bya//dang 
6b7(rgyud): po gtso bo skyed byed gang //hUM las nges par rdzogs bya zhing //gzhan rnams 'di las 'byung bar bsam//hUM maM baM aM//yaM baM DaM b+h+raM//pro maM saM shaM//pro shaM raM maM//de dag las byung sngags pa yis//'od dang 
6b8(rgyud): bcas par bde gshegs kyi//zhal nas 'byung bar yang dag ldan//de las rigs pa gang zhig gi//'bar 'phro nges par bskyed par bya//dang po kun kyang gcig pa yis//paM las gyur pa sna 
7a1(rgyud): @#//tshogs pa//yang dag par ni bskyed par byed//de 'og ka las rab bskyed pas//skram por yongs su brtag pa yin//de dag rab rdzogs sngags pa yis//sku ru yongs 
7a2(rgyud): su rdzogs byas la//bdag gis rnal 'byor yang dag 'grub//de nas sngags pa blo ldan pas//rigs su nges pa gang yin pa//thabs 'di la ni 'jug par bya//kun la 'jug par gyur pa 
7a3(rgyud): yi||khyab pa'i bdag po chen po ni//rigs kyi gtso bos spyi la khyab//mdun ni rdo rje'i rigs yin te//lha min la sogs nges par 'jig/g.yas pa nyid na gang gnas pa//rin chen 
7a4(rgyud): rigs su nges pa ste//sha za la sogs nges par 'jig/rgyab na gnas pa gang yin pa//pad+ma'i rigs su yang dag ldan//nor la sogs pa gang dgod pa//re ba bzhin du skong bar byed//
7a5(rgyud): g.yon na nges par gnas pa gang //las kyi rigs su nges pa las//klu dang gnod sbyin la sogs pa//ma rungs pa rnams yongs su 'jig/gzhan yang lhag ma thams cad kun//pho nya'i 
7a6(rgyud): tshul du nges par gnas//dang po nyi ma shar ba na//las kyi sbyor ba sna tshogs pa//so so dag la snying po nyid//gong du baM las rab rdzogs pa'i//brgya rtsa brgyad dag nges par bzlas//hUM gi sgra dang 
7a7(rgyud): rab ldan pas//bdag nyid kha ni me 'bar gsal//phyag rgya snang bar bcas byas te//de nyid stangs stabs bskyod pa yis//gdug pa thams cad sreg byed dang //bde gshegs mngon 
7a8(rgyud): par dgyes par bya//rdo rje dril bu gzung byas nas//khro bo'i cha lugs gang yin pas//phyogs rnams rmongs par byed pa yin//lha mo'i gzugs 'chang gang yin pas//gzhan gyi sems 
7b1(rgyud): kyang 'phrog par byed//re mig rnams kyi bar gang yin//me lce 'bar ba yongs su bri//'khor lo'i 'og tu gnas bya ba//chos 'byung phyag rgya nges par bri//thabs dang shes rab ldan pa la//kun 'byung 
7b2(rgyud): yum du nges par gsal//rten 'brel yang dag 'byung ba yis//kun rdzob la ni don dam brten//don dam nyid la kun rdzob kyang //brten pa nyid du yang dag gnas//de bas snang ba mi gsal bar//
7b3(rgyud): chos 'byung phyag rgya nges bar yang //rgyas pa la sogs ma yin gyi//drag po'i dkyil 'khor yin pa dang //de bzhin dbyibs su rang gsal ba//dran dang bcas pas bri bar bya//mthu dang ldan pa'i gang zag 
7b4(rgyud): la||stobs ni shin tu nges par skye//dkyil 'khor lha gnyis mngon bsgrubs pas//lus la gzi brjid 'jug pa'o//rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las dkyil 'khor dang lha'i le'u ste gsum pa'o//!//lag na rdo 
7b5(rgyud): rje gos sngon gyis//'jig rten mgon la 'di skad gsol//dkyil 'khor lha rnams dgyes bya ba//thabs de gang lags gsung du gsol//nang gi bdag nyid can dang yang //phyi yi khrus sogs rab 
7b6(rgyud): byas la//rab gsal la sogs rgyun bcad nas//gang yang rung ba'i lo nyid la//ston zla ra ba nas brtsams nas//lha min bla dang me tog gi/drag po dang po de nyid la//bdag gis sbyor ba nges par brtsam/
7b7(rgyud): de nas dang po bdag lus la//rgyan cha lnga'i rab brgyan te//lus la gos dag sngon po nyid//bla gos smad g.yogs yang dag bcings//spyi bo la sogs gnas lnga ru//sbrul gyis yongs su bcings par 
7b8(rgyud): bya||rang nyid dkyil 'khor shar sgo ru//'dug pa'i sbyor ba brtsam par bya//de nas dang po bla re gang //steng du nges par phub byas la//zur bzhi dag tu ba dan bzhi//sngon pos yongs su spyad par bya//
8a1(rgyud): @#//bla re dag gi dkyil dag nas//skud pa la rgyus me tog gi/phreng ba yongs su spyad par bya//de nas snga ma'i tshul gnas la//gong du bsten pa'i gnas rnams su//bdag gi las 'di 
8a2(rgyud): nges par brtsam//gtor ma'i las rnams kun rdzogs dang //mchod pa rnam pa lnga rnams dang //seng ldeng phur ba sor brgyad la//sngags 'di yis ni yang dag bsngags//oM badzra kI la ya 
8a3(rgyud): sarba big+h+nAn ha na ha na hUM phaT//oM badzra pany+tsa dz+nyA na sho d+ha ya sarba tsak+Shu kA ra ya hUM//sngags de dag gis phur bu dang //skud pa lnga la lan grangs ni//nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas byas la//tsan dan 
8a4(rgyud): la sogs dri lnga yis//gos par yang dag bcang bar bya//de nas sngags pa blo ldan pas//bdag nyid bsrung ba'i 'khor lo brtsam//rang gis phyi rol mtshams su ni//mi 'gyur las ni 
8a5(rgyud): bsil ldan bsgom//re steng sdud pa'i dngos po gang //kha dog nag po yang dag bsam//de las khyab byed rigs lnga pa//yang dag par ni bsam par bya//de nas yongs su rdzogs pa las//
8a6(rgyud): bdud rtsi 'khyil par bdag bsam ste//rdo rje sdig mdzub phyag rgya yis//sngags bzlas pas ni bgegs bskrad la//rigs gtso'i rtse mo de nyid la//kaM las lus skyob bsam bya ste//spyi 
8a7(rgyud): bor gnas pas khyab par bsam//de nas gtor ma de nyid la//bdug pa la sogs gang yin dang //skye ba bzhis bsdus sems can la//las kyi bar chad mi 'gyur phyir//bsam par 
8a8(rgyud): rdzogs par ring thang bya//de nas bdag nyid dad pa yis//mchod pa rnam pa lnga rnams ni//bsam pa sngags kyis dbul bar bya//gong ma'i stang stabs las bya ba//g.yas pa nges par 
8b1(rgyud): bskums pa las//g.yon pa legs par brkyang byas te//g.yas pa'i lag pas rdo rje nyid//phyar nas g.yon gyi be con dang //rtse sprad spyi bor gnas byas la//g.yon pa'i be con g.yas spos la//
8b2(rgyud): g.yas pa rdo rje g.yon pa yi//lag pas bzung nas snying gar mnan//be con nges par bskor ba yis//phyogs bzhi mtshams su nges par gdab//kha nas hUM gi sgra 'bar zhing //sna tshogs 
8b3(rgyud): rdo rje gar thabs kyis//lan gsum rab tu bskor bar bya//de nas gdug pa gang yin pa//phyogs bzhi dag tu song ba las//sa phyogs nges par btsang bar 'gyur//de nas thig sogs 
8b4(rgyud): gdab pa yis//sta gon legs par gnas pa dang //rim pa bzhin du dbang bskur la//rmi ltas nges par gdab par bya//bzang ngan bltas dag dbab pa dang //bzlog dang mi bzlog bya ba 
8b5(rgyud): gang //shes rab can gyis gsal bar brtag/sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor bzhengs bya'i phyir//bdag gis drag po dang po la//phye ma lnga la sngags dag dang //phyag rgya bcas pas byin gyis 
8b6(rgyud): brlab||de nas snod dag thod pa la//byas pa'i rdo rje phyag rgyas gdab//nges par las kyi sbyor ba tsam//phyogs su nges par bskor ba yin//dbugs rgyu ba yi dngos po gang //yi ge mngon par 
8b7(rgyud): gsal ba yis//rgyud 'di 'chad par bri bar bya//skabs kyi skad cig shes pa yis//rab tu khro gnyer bstan par bya//dang po nag po bri bya zhing //gnyis pa dkar por nges par dgye//gsum pa 
8b8(rgyud): nyid ni ser po ste//bzhi pa dmar po yang dag ldan//lnga pa ljang gu bri bya zhing //drug pa kha dog sna tshogs rgyas//dkyil 'khor las rnams rdzogs pa dang //rdzas su yongs su ldan byas te//gru bzhi dag du 
9a1(rgyud): @#//byang shar gyi//mtshams nas sa gang bu mo yis//blangs pa las byas mchod rten ni//sngon po bzhi dag gzhag par bya//de yi bskor ba'i rtse mo la//grong gi srad bu btags 
9a2(rgyud): byas te//me tog sngon po'i 'phreng ba yis//'khor bar yongs su brgyan par bya//bum pa sngon po bcu bdun nyid//mtha' ma'i phyogs su nges par gzhag/sman dang spos dang snying po 
9a3(rgyud): dang||lan tshwa rtsi thog la sogs pa'i//legs par bkang bas gnas pa yin//rgyan rnams kun gyis rab brgyan nas//lus la mngon par phyogs par bzhag/de nas sngags pa blo 
9a4(rgyud): ldan pa//grangs dang nges par rab sbyar la//zhal zas zur med legs pa dang //dri dang snang ba gang yin dang //byug pa'i dri dang tshangs skud dang //phreng ba ral gri be con rnams//yang 
9a5(rgyud): dag pa ru dgod par bya//gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang //phyogs su nges par yang dag gnas//de nas bdag po gang zhig gis//na bza' dar yug sngon po la//gur gum 
9a6(rgyud): thig les legs bris la//de nyid la ni dbul bar bya//yol ba sngon pos phyogs bskor la//phyi ma rnams kyis snang mi bya//gal te snang bar gyur pa na//rang gi lus las ma gtogs 
9a7(rgyud): pa'i||gzhan rnams brlag par 'gyur ba yin//de bas legs par bsgrims pa yis//phyi rol gzhan la bstan mi bya//gang zag gcig pur gnas pa yis//las kyi sbyor ba 'di brtsam 
9a8(rgyud): bya||phyag dag sngon du 'ong ba yis//mchod pa'i las la mngon par gzhag/bdag nyid las kyi phyag rgyar gnas//bsam pa mngon du gyur pa yis//kun gyi 'bras bu gang yin pa//bdag 
9b1(rgyud): gis gsal bar rab tu brtag/sngags dang phyag rgya rab ldan pa'i//mchod pa 'di dag nges pa las//bgegs bsal ye shes rab dbab pa'i//rnal 'byor rdzogs par yang dag gnas//
9b2(rgyud): rang rang rigs kyi phyag rgya yis//sngags bzlas pa yis dbul bar bya//oM na mo nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Da la de ba hA nAM/pu sh+pa pU dzA me b+ha sa mu drA s+pha ra Na sa ma ye hUM/oM na mo nI lAM pa ra d+ha ra 
9b3(rgyud): badzra pA Ni maN Da la d+ha ra yA mi hUM/oM na mo ni lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra bA Ni maN Da la de ba tA nAM/oM lo ge ni ra ya ta yA mi hUM/oMn mo nI lAM ba ra d+d+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN+Dal de ba tA nAM/
9b4(rgyud): ga na d+ha ni ra yA ta yA mi hUM/o na mo nI lAM ba rad+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Ta la de ba tA nAM/nai we d+ye ni ra yA ta yA mi hwUM/oM na mo nIlAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pa Ni maN Da la de ba ta nAM/sha b+ta ni ra yA ta yA mi hu+AM/oM na mo 
9b5(rgyud): ni lAM ba ra d+ha ra badza pA Ni maN Da la de pa tA nAM/tsa ri yA ni ra yA ta yA mi hA hUM/oM na mo na lI lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Da la de ba hA nAM/ha ri ni ra yA ta yA mi ha hUM/oM na mo nI lAM bar d+ha ra badzra pA Ni man Da la de ba tA 
9b6(rgyud): nAM|bA sa na nir yA ta yA mi hUM /oM na mo nI lAM pa ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni me Na Da la de ba tA nAM/ga na d+ha ri nir yA ta yA mi hA+A+uM/oM na mo nI mI bar d+ha ra badzra pA Ni mi Na Da la de ba tA nA/s+pha ra sha nir yA ta yA mi hA/oM na 
9b7(rgyud): mo nI lAM bar d+ha rabadzra pA Ni maN Da la da ba tA nA//rat+na sarba bstu ni ra yA ta yA mi hrUM//de yis sngags kyi mchod pa las//sdig pa bshags pa'i sngags kyis su//phyag rgya nges par rab bcas pa//sdig 
9b8(rgyud): pa bshags pa'i smre sngags kyis//lus ngag yid gsum btud pa yis//yang dag par ni bshags par bya//bsod nams rjes su yi rang dang //yongs su bsngo dang skyabs 'gro dang //sems 
10a1(rgyud): @#//bskyed pa dang gnas bzhi dang //gong tu bstan pa'i phyag rgya nyid//skabs 'di dag tu shes par bya//dang po rgyu las skyes pa las//phyi nas dngos por mi gnas shing //rang bzhin 
10a2(rgyud): 'od ni gsal bar sgom//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las mchod pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o//!//de nas lag na rdo rje yis//gsang ba'i mgon la 'di skad gsol//bsam 
10a3(rgyud): pa rab tu rdzogs pa yi//nang gi rnal 'byor gsung du gsol//bde gshegs gsang bas bka' stsal pa//gang zhig bsgrub par bya ba la//thabs kyi dngos grub 'grub par 'gyur//dang po sh+se 
10a4(rgyud): rab can dag gis//bdag gis dngos por snang ba las//de nyid nga ral mchog nyid kyis/'od zer bsil ldan yang dag bsam//de la gang du gyur pa ni//dbugs rgyu 'gugs par nges par 
10a5(rgyud): bas na//de las gsal ldan rigs lnga pa//stong pa'i dpe las mngon song ba'i//gzugs bzangs ma sogs rigs ma yis//mi 'chi lnga ldan rus 'gyur las//'dzin pa can la sbyin par bsgom//
10a6(rgyud): de nas sngags 'dis rab bsngags te//bdag nyid rigs su ldan pa yis//bdag pos phyag rgya nges par bsam//oM a b+hi Shiny+tsa tu mAM badzra bA ni hUM/a ho A ha raM ta tha s+thi to yaM a ho ma ma bri 
10a7(rgyud): yang tu ti hUM//bdag nyid dngos po la brten nas//mtshan mar mi gnas ma skyes pa'i//gdod nas grub pa bsam bya ste//sangs rgyas dngos la rab sbyor bas//dang por dngos dag bsam 
10a8(rgyud): byas nas//bdag nyid gong gi gzugs gnas la//steng du mig 'dra'i dngos po bsgom//de las gong du gyur pa las//'jigs byed rigs dgu nges par bsam//mig 'dra'i sbu gu rab sbyar 
10b1(rgyud): bas||snying po'i steng du gsal bar snang //snying po'i steng du chu bsgrubs pas//yan lag stong dang ldan pa nyid//spun la phyogs par kha bltas nas//bdag las gong du gyur pa yi//hUM gnyis las 
10b2(rgyud): bsgrubs steng ma yi//nges par gsal byed sbyar ba las//dngos po'i snying por rab song ste//de las mi 'chi 'od ldan pas//'joms byed steng na rab gnas pa'i//mig 'dra'i yan lag brgyad dang 
10b3(rgyud): ldan||'joms byed mig 'dra gar mar sbyor//thabs dang shes rab gsum du nges//dngos po gsum las rab brgyud de//snying po'i steng na rab gnas pa'i|/mig 'dra'i steng du nges par song //
10b4(rgyud): rang gi gar ma'i khyon tsam la//skad cig bye brag bcu drug pa'i//thabs su yongs su rdzogs pa ste//dang po dngos por brtag mi bya//rdzogs pa nyid la mi 'bar bas//rim pa de nyid mi gnas 
10b5(rgyud): shing||dngos po'i snang ba gang yin pa//mar me'i rtse mo lta bu yi//phyi nas rang bzhin 'od gsal ba'i//dang po rab tu brtag par bya//rim pa gsum pa rab rdzogs yin//steng du paM gnyis rab 
10b6(rgyud): 'bar dang //hUM las skyes pa thub pa'i sku//yang dag pa ru brtag bya yin//'og kyang gong ma'i sa bon las//bskyed par bya ba kho na yin//sems dpa' gnyis su nges pa dang //'khor lo 
10b7(rgyud): spros pas char lta bu'i//lo tog shin tu smin byed pa'i//skyon rnams spangs nas de nyid du//slar yang ngo bos yang dag gnas//de ltar gsal bar ma 'khrul bar//skyon gyis ma gos dri med do//
10b8(rgyud): mkha' la kun zhes sems kyis su//rdo rje lam du rab zhugs nas//nor bu dri med zla ba yi//las la sbyong ba sna tshogs pa//mnyam par sbyar bas bde ba'i mchog/ma 'dzag gnas dang 
11a1(rgyud): @#//ldan par 'gyur//mkhas pa sangs rgyas bde bas bde//lus la chags par mi lta zhing //sems la chags pa chen por bskyed//de 'dra'i spyod pas yongs bsdus nas//
11a2(rgyud): gang zhig mya ngan 'das pa las//mchog 'gyur mi gnas mya ngan 'das//sa la mngon par mi gnas shing //kun tu'ang mngon par mi gnas pas//ye shes rdo rje go 'phang nyid//brgyud 
11a3(rgyud): pas mngon du byed pa la//'di la rdzas la sogs pa yi//'di yi dngos grub gang yin pas//nges par 'grub pa smos ci dgos//shes rab can gyi las 'di las//chags pa chen por yang 
11a4(rgyud): dag bsgrub//lus la me 'bar chen po yi//snying po 'di nyid bzlas pa las//rang nyid padam gsang ba yi//drag po'i rtags su nges pa ste//de bzhin rtag tu bsgrub par bya//sngags bzlas 
11a5(rgyud): cho ga gsum las ni//khyad par nges par dmigs byas pa//bdag nyid tha ma la gzugs kyis gnas//de dag phan tshun brgyud pa yis//zo chun brgyud pa bzhin du bsam//
11a6(rgyud): drag po'i bzlas pa de nyid dang //rang bzhin rdo rje'i bzlas pa yis//rigs gsum yongs su bsdus pa yin//oM b+ho b+ho ban nA ga a d+hi pa ta ye//i dan nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra sha rI raM//
11a7(rgyud): badzra pA Ni hri da yaM ya/sa mA te kra ma tha mA ku la u da sA da nAM b+ha bi ShaM te tad+ya thA/bud d+ha bud d+ha/bi bud d+ha ma ti kra ma thA/ha ra ha ra nA gA nAM/pra ha ra pra ha ra nA gA nAM/ts+tshin da ts+tshin da nA ga 
11a8(rgyud): hri da yA ni/b+hin da b+hin da/nA ga marmA Ni bi shI rlan+tu nA ga sha raM raM/bi swo Da ya nA ga nA ya nA ni/tad+ya thA hUM phaT/nA ga bi dA ra NA ya hUM phaT/nA god sA da nA ya hUM phaT/nI lAM ba sa nA ya hUM phaT/a pra te ha ta ba la ba ra kra 
11b1(rgyud): ma ya hUM phaT/kri tAn tA ya hUM phaT/kri dAn ta ru pA ya hUM phaT/ro Sha NA ya hUM phaT/trAs nA ya hUM phaT/kuM b+hAN Da ud sA da nA ya phaT/prA+e ta pi shA tsi bi nA sha ka rA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha a pa smA bi nA sha/
11b2(rgyud): rA ya phaT/ha na ha na hUM phaT/ha sa ha sa/da ra da ra/mA ra ya mA ra ya/nA sha ya nA sha ya/sarba bi g+hi bi nA sha ka rA ya phaT/sarba sha trUM saM g+ha bi nA sha ka rA ya hruM phaT/i dan nI lA ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA 
11b3(rgyud): Ni rA dzA pa ya ti swA hA/i daMSh+TA ka rA lo kra b+hi Sha Na nI lA ba ra d+ha ra padzra pA Ni ye nA ga a laM kA ra sa bi ta ke tu byA g+h+yA tsa ra ma ni b+hA sa nA yA/b+ha ma kA rA ga ru Ta ra si ti/kro d+ha mAr ti ni trai lo kya da hA ya/b+h+ya 
11b4(rgyud): ma man tA a sa na ru dra ya/na ga tse tur ma b+hak+ShA ya hUM phaT/oM ma trai lo gya bi dza ya hUM phaT/oMm hA kA la ro Sha NA ya hUM phaT/oMbai shra ra ma nA ya hUM phaT/oMa mri ta kuN Ta la he na ha na hUM phaT/oM badzra 
11b5(rgyud): yak+Sha hUM phaT/oM badzrA ya bai tA lI ni hUM phaT/oM badzrar+Sha kA ya hUM phaT/oM badzrab+hai ra bA ya hUM phaT/oMM badzra pra b+hota ba tA ya hUM phaT/oMmano ha ra ya hUM phaT/oMs man ta b+hA d+yai hUM phaT/oM sha ba da gI t+yai 
11b6(rgyud): hUM phaT/oM pra NA pa te t+yai hUM phaT/oM r+n+rAMs t+yai hUM phaT/o ra ka ta prA yo ni hUM phaT/oMmAn sa b+ha k+Shi Ni hUM phaT/oM tsaN Da ma hA ro Sha Na sarba sid d+hA ya hUM phaT/oMn ma ni d+ha ri badzra Ni hUM phaT/o bA su 
11b7(rgyud): ra ta na bi ta hUM phaT/oM a mri ta si da d+hi hUM phaT/oMsam sho d+ha ya ba ra ba ra hUM phaT/oM tsa ba sha sa ba ni ra hUM phaTaHoM ku mAr tsa sha ma Ni hUM phaT//de nas phyogs bcu 'khrugs pa las//yi 
11b8(rgyud): ge nges pa gang yin pa//bla ma'i man ngag rab gsal bas//gcig tu bsdus pas bzlas par bya//rab byung sngags pa'i bdag nyid la//grangs tshad gzung ba gang yin pa//rigs 
12a1(rgyud): @#//kyi dbang gis rnam par bshad//kun tu snang ba gang yin pa//rigs su bsdus nas dag gi yang //spyod yul ma yin tha mal gnyid//drag po'i snying po nyid dang yang//
12a2(rgyud): rang bzhin rdo rje bzlas pa yis//rigs gsum yang dag bsdus pa yin//gong ma'i sngags la rab sngags pa//mtha' ru kun la hUM gis brgyan//de mthar bskrad pa'i tshig dag gis 
12a3(rgyud): yongs su brgyan pas nges par bzlas//gang dang gang la las sbyor ba//de dang de las man ngag gis//mngon par shes pa'i yang dag bshad//bzlas pa phun sum tshogs ldan pas//
12a4(rgyud): rab 'bring tha ma ldan pa yis//dang po sbyor ba nges pa dang //mtha' mar las la 'jug pa la//grangs dang cha ru rnam par ldan//spyod yul bsdus pa nges gsal ba//bdag gis gang la brtag par bya//
12a5(rgyud): dang por srid la chags pa dang //bar du gang zhig gnas pa nyid//tha mar 'jig pa gang yin pas//sngags rnams thams cad rgyas par gsungs//'byung ba lnga dang rab ldan nas//de las chos rnams 
12a6(rgyud): thams cad las//yongs su 'byung ba'i yang dag gnas//de dang rab sbyor dang po'i don//'di las grub par gyur pa gang //las rnams sna tshogs gang yin pa//gdon mi za bar nges pa yin//lag na rdo rje 
12a7(rgyud): gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las nang dang sngags kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o////lag na rdo rje gos sngon gyis//bcom ldan mgon la 'di skad gsol//sems can smin par bya ba'i phyir//'jug pa'i thabs 
12a8(rgyud): nyid gsung du gsol//kun mkhyen mgon pos bka' stsal pa//dang por sa brtag la sogs pa//nges par gong du bstan pa ltar//las rnams nges par rdzogs par bya//sa phyogs dben 
12b1(rgyud): pa'i khyim rnams su//ba yi rnam lnga la sogs dang //ram dang mthing shun mdun du nges par sbyang //tha ma'i tshad du rab sbyar la//gung gum thigs les yang dag gdab//zhal zas sna tshogs 'byor ldan 
12b2(rgyud): pa'i//kun kyang lnga ltar yongs su gnas//las rab mchog gi gtor ma la//rdzas rnams phun sum tshogs pa yis//sems bskyed pa dang skyabs 'gro dang //gsang ba gsum gyi gnas rnams 
12b3(rgyud): su//dri yi thig le rdo rje dang //bcas pa'i gung mos kun bskus la//mi 'gyur gsum la sku gsum du//sngags 'di dag gis byin gyis rlobs//oM tri ma ta bi sho d+ha ya A hUM//de nas so shing 
12b4(rgyud): dag pa ni//sngags 'dis yongs su dag byas la//slob ma la byin so ba khrus te//mig bkab pa la bdag pa yis//de yi steng du bor bar bya//gang du kha bltas me tog phreng //rigs dang rjes su 
12b5(rgyud): bstan pa bas//de yi dngos grub brjod par bya//de nas srung skud sngon po la//sngags kyis dag par byas pa las//skyes pa g.yas pa bud med g.yon//dpung pa dag la bcing bar bya//bkra shis rtswa ye 
12b6(rgyud): sngas dang ston//legs par btsugs pa'i mtshan ma brtag/bzang ngan ltas su bcas pa dang //bzlog par bya dang bya mi dgos//shes rab can gyis rgyud gzhan las//'byung ba bzhin du brtags 
12b7(rgyud): par bya//dang por bsten pa rab 'bring dang //tha mar yongs su bsnyen byas la//de 'og sngags pa blo ldan pas//yang dag par ni sbyor ba nga rtsam//oM ba dzra sa ma ya sid d+hi A//oM sarba rak+Sha sarba sid 
12b8(rgyud): d+ha A hUM//rang bzhin mi gnas dgos pa yis//kun rdzob dri ma sbyang bar bya//de nas sna tshogs 'byor ba las//gong du smos pa'i dkyil 'khor nyid//yongs su rgyas par bri bar bya//
13a1(rgyud): @#//rdzas rnams phun sum tshogs pa la//mchod pa yang dag mchod par bya//de nyid gzugs la rab tu yang //gnas pas bdag nyid de nyid du//yang dag par ni bskyed 
13a2(rgyud): par bya//pad+ma nyid la gos la sogs//sngon pos yang dag dkris byas la//bdud brtsi 'khyil ba'i sngags kyis su//dri chus gtor zhing gzhug par bya//mdun du nges par gnas 
13a3(rgyud): pa las//che chung de nyid 'byor ba yis//yon nyid khas dag len du gzhug/thal mo bdag gis bzung byas la//sems bskyed la sogs rgyas par brjod//snang la sna tshogs nges pa 
13a4(rgyud): las//rigs ni rjes su gzung bar bya//me tog sngon po'i phreng ba yis//sngags brjod pas ni bor bar bya//de ltar lha tshogs rab bstan nas//bum pa'i chu yi blob ma la//dbang bskur 
13a5(rgyud): slob dpon bdag nyid kyis//byin pa bum pa'i dbang yin te//rags pa rnam par dag par 'dod//de nas phyag rgya bcas pa bas//byang chub sems ldan ma nyams pas//bdag dang gzhan rnams 
13a6(rgyud): gang rung bas//bde ba mngon du rab sbyar nas//bsam pas yang dag sbyar byas te//thabs dang shes rab phyag rgya yis//de yi kha ru sbyin par bya//de dag gsang ba'i dbang yin te//de 'og rnam 
13a7(rgyud): par dag pa yin//de nas bdag nyid dngos po gang //bu mo mdog bzang sngon mo la//gang dang ldan pa'i yang ba'i bskyod//tshogs yang dag bzung byas la//thabs gnyis rab tu bskyod pa yis//
13a8(rgyud): sna tshogs dngos po snang ba yis//de 'og sor bzhi gzhal byas nas//shes rab gnyis la mnan pa yis//bde ba dpag tu med pa myong //de myong skad cig rdzogs pa yis//dpe la bltas pa 
13b1(rgyud): yin par bstan//de yis shes rab yes shes kyi//dbang nyid mngon par rdzogs par 'gyur//de rdzogs sems nyid dag pa de//bzhi pa chos nyid mngon rtogs dbang //med par mngon par snang 
13b2(rgyud): bar 'gyur//de yis spyi la khyab gnas te//rang la yod bzhin nges par spangs//oM A khaM bI ra hUM/oM ya tha ku la ta thA a haM/sangs rgyas kun gyi phyag brnyan 'bar//de nyid du ni slob ma 'grub//
13b3(rgyud): dar la sogs pa sngon po yis//slob ma nyid la dbang bskur la//sngags 'di yis ni byin rlabs la//dang po gnyis sbyar dang bzung nas//sngags kyis yang dag bskon par bya//mkha' lding sngon po 
13b4(rgyud): 'dab rgyas pa//mgul ba dar dbyangs ldan pa yis//bu nyid la ni nges par sbyin//dngos grub sna tshogs 'grub pa dang //gdon rnams thams cad 'joms pa dang //be con sngon po dbyig dang ldan//
13b5(rgyud): me tog sngon po'i phreng bas kyang //de nyid la ni bskon byas nas//sngags dang phyag rgya 'dir ldan pa'i//bu nyid la ni sbyin par bya//dang po gsum pa rab sbyar bas//spyi bo'i steng du nges bzung ste//de ni 
13b6(rgyud): byed pa po la bzhag/bdag nyid yan lag rigs ngan gyis//dbus kyi g.yon rab grang byas la//rigs tshang yan lag bcings pa yis//phyir te byed pa po la bzhag/de yi spyod la gnas bya ba//
13b7(rgyud): tshangs pa chen po mthong byed dang //mchod pa kun gyis sdud pa yis//dar dmar ras sam gang rung ba'i//sngags chus dag byas legs bkab nas//bzhi ru nges par gcig pa yi//dngos po gang 
13b8(rgyud): dag gdags par bya//phyag rgya 'da' min grags par ldan//byis pas sngags bzlas yang dag ldan//kun la khro gnyer brjid par blta//oM u da ra saM g+ha ba b+hu ya hUM/oM ga ru Ta sarba nA ga b+hak+Sha 
14a1(rgyud): @#//ya hUM/oM ba raM dan ta ka sarba sid d+hi prA ba ta hUM/oM adz+nyo pa ya ti lo ka pa shaM ka rA ya hUM/de yi lag pa sgrom byas la//bdag gis drag po'i las sbyor ba//de yi sgrom nas 
14a2(rgyud): drangs byas la//snying po 'di nyid nges par sbyin//oM tsaN Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra pA ni da ha da ha sarba big+h+nan ha na ha na sarba ar+shA shI g+h+raM ku ru ye hUM/de nas legs pa'i mtshan ma la//chos la dbang sogs 
14a3(rgyud): mi gnas na//'jug par 'gyur ba ga ya srid//rgyud gzhan la ni skyid bang gnas//de nas kun la 'jug par 'gyur//spyi nyid dbang la mi ltos par//de nyid la ni rtog pa'i mchog/
14a4(rgyud): 'di yis 'jug pa kho na yin//lan na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las dbang gi le'u ste drug pa'o//!//de nas lag na rdo rje yis//gsang ba'i mgon la 'di skad gsol//thams 
14a5(rgyud): cad rdzogs pa chen po yis//rim pa'i rnal 'byor gsung du gsol//bde gshegs gsang bas bka' stsal pa//steng dang 'og gi steng du gnyis//dkyil 'khor bzhi yi bdag nyid ni//thams 
14a6(rgyud): cad shes rab dam pa ste//yon tan sdud sogs tshogs snying po//sa bon 'bras bu rnam pa yis//spar dang bsgom la ltung bar 'grub//rnam par snang mdzad gnas dag tu//rtse gnyis gnyis 
14a7(rgyud): kyi bdag nyid can//glo gtang rtse la ltung gyur nas//bde de gyen du bzlog pas ni//thams cad gnas su gcig tu 'gyur//snang ba mtha' yas gnas dag tu//brgyad gnyis shes rab ri 
14a8(rgyud): mor bcas//dbus kyi byang chub thig le la//glo gtang snang mdzad gnas su phyin//de yis sangs rgyas thams cad bkug/yid bzhin nor bu dang 'dar 'gyur//'og nas rang gi gnas su bsdu//
14b1(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i gnas dag tu//brgyad dang bzhi yi bdag nyid can//dang po sa dang 'byung ba yis//nges pa'i ri mo bsam par bya//rang gis nis mtha' yas bskul//'dres pa'i bde ba'i 
14b2(rgyud): gnas su byin//thams cad 'dres por rang gi sar//bde dang ldan nas de yi 'grub//sprul pa'i gnas sa bcu gsum la//gnyis pa gnyis dang yang dag ldan//yi ge thig le bcas pa 
14b3(rgyud): yis||'gro rnams bde ba'i gnas su spros//slar 'dus da ru dngos grub 'gyur//yang na rtag chad bral ba yi//dbu ma chen po'i dbyings nyid du//ye shes phra mo mar gyis spros//dbyings 
14b4(rgyud): la thug nas mar la byung //de tshe mi gnas bsam dang bral//'dod chags dag dang ldan gyur na//gcig shos lon pa'i yon tan ldan//rtse mor ma dad bcu ldan pas//tsan Da li yi drod byung nas//
14b5(rgyud): sems dpa' ye shes rtse la zung //de yis yang sprul gnas su bkug/snyoms par zhugs shing g.yo ba'i bzlog/tha ma'i yi ge 'gro 'ong ni//zhu ba'i bsam pa'i bya bar gsungs//de nas de 
14b6(rgyud): bsad dngos grub 'gyur//yang ni 'og gi thig le bzhi//rlung de sprul pa'i 'khor lor 'dus//dro bar byang chub sems ga kug nas//rtse la phir nas nub byang bsam//slar log ngar 'gro yang dang 
14b7(rgyud): yang||tha ma snyoms par zhugs grub 'gyur//yang na gnas bzhi gsum po dag/gang yang rung bas steng du phyin//slar log rdo rje rtse la bton//tshul log gnas nas las rnams byas//nges nas 
14b8(rgyud): dngos grub chen po yang //rdo rje bzhin du 'grub par bsam//khams gsum thams cad dag pa dang //zhing rnams gtsang mar skye bar 'gyur//bsam gtan de dang ldan pa'i tshe//gzhan pa'i 
15a1(rgyud): @#//yul du 'grub 'gyur spang //spang ba ma yin blang ba min//snyoms pa de yi ngang la bzhag/gzugs dang sgra la sogs pa yi//rang gi sems g.yeng 'gyur ba'i tshe//bstan 
15a2(rgyud): pa'i rang bzhin med pa bsam//bar du so so'i thig le bsam//thams cad sgyu ma lta bur brtag/dang po dang po 'dus dag tu//'dod pa'i yon tan lha mor bsam//de nas rtog pa zhi bar 
15a3(rgyud): 'gyur//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las thams cad rdzogs pa chen po'i rim pa rnal 'byor pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas lag na rdo rje yis//bcom ldan 
15a4(rgyud): mgon la 'di skad gsol//'jig rten kun la phan gdags phyir//gtor ma'i rim pa gsung du gsol//kun mkhyen mgon gyis bka' stsal pa//rnal 'byor rnams la phan gdags 
15a5(rgyud): phyir||bdag gis gtor ma bshad par bya//phan gdags pa dang tshar gcad pa//phan pa mchog dang thun mong ngo//gsang ba bla med mchog tu 'dod//phyi nang thun mong shes par 
15a6(rgyud): bya||rgyas dang bsdus dang shin tu bsdus//tshar gcod pa yang shes par bya//dgug dang bskrad dang gsad par dang //gnan dang bsreg par shes par bya//rgyun dus chen po'i 
15a7(rgyud): gsum nyid dang //chung du rgyun nam dus chen gyi//bcu gnyis bzhi'am tha ma gcig/rtsa ba lnga 'am yan lag lnga//'bru dang shing tog sman rnams dang //chang 'thun 'bur ba la sogs 
15a8(rgyud): rdzas||de stengs dkar gsum klu la shis//khrag sna snyi sna sha sna gsum//de sogs mthun rdzas tshar gcad pa'i//bskyed rim dang ni ldan pa yis//bla nas bsrung ba byas pa las//
15b1(rgyud): chog gsum du byas pa'am//yang na skad cig lhar bskyed la//mtshan dang ldan pa'i pad+ma ru//rdzas rnams kun gyis bkang nas ni//zla ba rdo rje hUM gis mtshan//mtha gang la ni ri bong can//
15b2(rgyud): de la bcu drug bdun pa'i mtshan//rdo rje mtshan ldan ba ru ni//sdud spyod 'phyo ba'i mthing dmar dkar//pad 'od ye shes lnga ldan gsum//gru gsum 'og nas zla gam can//thugs kyi mthing 
15b3(rgyud): nag 'phro ba'i las//rdo rje lte bskul mtha' gsum bskyod//dmar po 'bar bas mtshan ldan khrol//thugs kyis bsdus shing gsung gis sbyangs//sku las 'phros pa'i ri 'ong bskul//shes 
15b4(rgyud): rab can las 'od zer 'phros//de yis yab yum bskul nas ni//kha dog dkar po'i bdud rtsi 'bab//dang po skul byed 'od du zhu//khong seng gnas pa pad mar 'bab//bab pa'i gting la de 
15b5(rgyud): nyid ltung //lan gsum bskor bas 'og ma bzhin//bzhi pa'i dang po gnyis par mtshan//de nas gdan bskyed 'khor bcas bkug/ji baM rdo rje khong seng gyis//bzhi pa'i dang po gnyis? par 
15b6(rgyud): bsam||yang na yud tsam lhar bsams la//thugs kar nyi ma de steng hUM//'od kyis chos 'byung gru gsum sprul//de yis nang du pad+ma ni//'dab brgyad dur khrod de dbus su//yaM las rlung gyur raM 
15b7(rgyud): las me//de steng a las ka pA la//nang du bdud rtsi oM AHhUM//da steng zla ba rdo rje hUM//zla aM 'od 'phros bdud rtsi bkug/bsdus dang gzhu dang bdud rtsir bsam//yi ge gsum dang go rim bzhin//
15b8(rgyud): thugs las 'od 'phros rlung la phog/rlung gi me spar thong pa bskol//de yi rkyen gyis bsdus pa dang //gzhu dang mdur rtser bsdus pa dang //yi ge gsum dang sbyar la dbul//yang na lha mo dkar mo yis//
16a1(rgyud): @#//mtshan ldan dang ni dkar la mchod//yang na skad cig slar bskyed la//thugs ka'i hUM gis bdud rtsi sbyangs//slar yang ye shes bdud rtsi bab//yud tsam sprul pas lha yis gsog/
16a2(rgyud): bstod nas gshegs shing las bcol lo//rgyas dang bsdus dang shin tu bsdus//kha zas sbyang ba bsdus dang 'thun//de ltar yi ge brgya pas gshegs//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje 
16a3(rgyud): me lce'i rgyud las gtor ma'i cho ga'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o//!//de nas rdo rje gos sngon gyis//bcom ldan mgon po 'di skad gsol//rnal 'byor thams cad bkra shis dang //gnod pa thams 
16a4(rgyud): cad bsal ba'i phyir//sbyin bsreg cho ga gsung du gsol//'jig rten mgon gyis bka' stsal pa//gzhan dag dran pa bskyed pa dang //dngos grub ldan pa thob 'dod pas//man ngag rim par 
16a5(rgyud): dran pa'i phyir//rnal 'byor don du bshad par bya//sngon du bsnyen pa byas pa yis//bsgrub pa yi ni dus dag du//sbyin bsreg gis ni las kun 'grub//sa brtag bslang zhing sbyang ba dang //byin 
16a6(rgyud): gyis brlabs cing bsrung ba dang //sa gzhi dkar por rgyar khru gang //zab sor bcu gnyis zlum dor bya//de dbus rdo rje rtse lnga pa//sor ni brgyad pa dag tu byas//thab de ba yi rnam lnga dang //nang 
16a7(rgyud): kun dri ni dkar por byug/dus gsum me tog dkar por gtor//yaM shing nim pa pa la sha/u dum ba ra a mra la//sogs pa byang chub shing dag dang //'dab ma can dang 'o ma can//rlan 
16a8(rgyud): dang bcas pa mnyam bcad la//rtse mo'i cha las byung ba yi//mtho gang tsam ni 'o ma dang //mar dang sbrang rtsi la sogs pa//rtse gnyis lo ma bcas pa 'bul//rnying rul gas dang 'bras 
16b1(rgyud): zos pa//srog chags gnas pa spang bar bya//nas dang 'bras pos til dag dang //'bras chan yungs kar mar dang zho//me tog thang chu dri zhim dang //ci tsam rnyed pa 'bul bar bya'o//bud shing dag gi 
16b2(rgyud): rnam pa yang //thabs las cung zad dma' bar ni//dgon pa'i ba yi lci bas bya//de nas sngags pa lus bkrus te//dri zhim spos dang bla bas byug/tshad med bzhi yi sems ldan zhing //zhi 
16b3(rgyud): ba'i rgyan bcas dkar po bgos//lha yi rnal 'byor gsal byas la//srod dam snga dro'i dus su ni//shar phyogs su ni kha bltas la//zhi ba sems kyis zhi ba bya//mchod yon 'bru dang bcas pa yi//
16b4(rgyud): dri zhim pa yi chus byug la//rang gi lha yi snying po brjod//las thams cad pa'i chu dang ni//rdo rje gnod sbyin sngags kyis gsang //las rnams thams cad bum pa dang //mchod yon g.yon du gzhag par 
16b5(rgyud): bya//bud shing bsreg rdzas g.yas su gzhag/de nas oM badzra yak+Sha hUM/zhes pas me ni sbar ba ste//rlung yab kyis ni me sbar ro//oM dz+wa la dz+wa la hUM//de nas me sbar snying po yin//de nas 
16b6(rgyud): hUM gis thab bskyed la//de dbus raM las gru gsum me//oM las pad+ma la gnas pa//raM las byung ba'i me lha ni//sku mdog dkar po rgyan dang ldan//'bar ba'i 'phreng bas rab tu brgyan//
16b7(rgyud): gsus khyim che la phreng ba g.yon//g+han te 'dzin pa'i rdo rje me//rang lha'i rnal 'byor ngad ldan pas//thugs ka'i sa bon 'od zer gyis//me lha ye shes sems dpa' bkug/bkug cing dbang 
16b8(rgyud): du bya ba'o//de nas dbang ni bskur ba dang //sku gsung thugs byin brlab pa'o//de nas mchod yon phul nas ni//mchod pa lngas ni rab mchod do//sgeg mo la sogs mchod pa'i mchod//
17a1(rgyud): @#//oM ag+na ye ha ba byA ka byA ba ha na ya ha bya ti ya na ye swA hA//zhes pas bsreg rdzas rnams 'bul bya//de nas 'bul ba'i rim pa ni//'bar ba'i mel yam shing ste//de   
17a2(rgyud): nas mar dang de 'og til//de nas 'bras chen la sogs pa'o//de nas bstod de las bcol lo//de nas me yi dbus rang gis//lhag pa'i lha nyid rim pa bzhin//dbang bskur ba dang bsam 
17a3(rgyud): par bya//sku gsung thugs su byin gyis brlabs//de yang mchod yon zhabs bsil phul//mchod pa rnam pa lnga dag dang //sgeg mo la sogs pa rnams dbul//de nas phyag na rdo rje ni//
17a4(rgyud): gos sngon lce la hUM bsams la//oM tsaN Da ma hA ro Sha Na hUM phaT/shAn ti ku ru swA hA zhes snga ma'i rim pa bzhin du bya//bsreg rdzas re re la bdun bdun//yang na gsum gsum 'bul bar bya/
17a5(rgyud): bzlas mthar sbyin bsregs bya bar gces//de la mar gyi sbyin bsregs dbul//yang na ma grugs nas dag dang //yungs kar mar dang sbyar la dbul//so ba mar dang yungs kar la//
17a6(rgyud): sogs pas las kyi sgrib pa sbyang //de phyir nyi shu rtsa gcig gam//yang na rgya rtsa brgyad du dbul//de nas mchod yon dang 'thor 'thung phul la bstod par bya'o//oM ye shes chen po'i    
17a7(rgyud): khyad pa las//sprul pa phyag na rdo rje ste//mthon mthing sku 'dra 'od zer 'bar//bdud 'dul khro gtum brjid pa'i zhal//phyag g.yas rdo rje rtse lnga bsnams//dril bus g.yon gyi dkur   
17a8(rgyud): brten 'gying //sku ni brgyan dang na bzas brgyan//phyag brnyan bye ba'i tshogs kyis bskor//de la bstod de phyag 'tshal lo//de nas yang me lha snga ma bzhin du mchod la bstod do//drag 
17b1(rgyud): bo maN Dal gru gsum la khru gang tshad las phyed brkos te//lho phyogs zur bstan gsum ni dor//bdud rtsi la sogs nag por bya//de dbus rdo rje steng spangs te//bar du me ri rtse mo 'phags//bud shing 
17b2(rgyud): gnag cing gdug pa'i shing //thab kyi tshad las zor mtho tsam//yam shing de 'thun bcu gnyis tshad//mchod rdzas nag po 'ba' zhig go/bsreg rdzas mar nag rus khrag sha//skye tshe shang tshe yungs 
17b3(rgyud): dkar dang //de la sogs pa'i mthu rdzas dang //de rnams rdzas la gzugs brnyan bya//mchod rdzas g.yas la bsrog rdzas g.yon//byin gyis brlab cing gzhag par bya//dgang gzar blugs gzar 
17b4(rgyud): gzhan dang mthun//yang na lag pa dag gis bya//de gnyis rdo rje dril bu yang //mdun nyid du ni gzhag par bya//las kyi bum pa'i thams cad bsang //dus ni nam byed nyin phyed bya//nag po rgyan gon 
17b5(rgyud): lho phyogs blta//g.yon brkyang 'dug ste drag du bzlas//mche ba brtsigs shing khro gnyer bsdus//dang po bsnyen pa sngon song bas//me pra brtag cing bgegs bzlog bya//bzang na de ni bya mi dgos//
17b6(rgyud): thab bsang bya zhing bskyed pa dang //cho ga gsum mam skad cig mas//me lha nag po khros pa bskyed//ye shes me lha spyan drangs bstim//gnyis su med par rgyas btab la//me lha'i sngags mchod 
17b7(rgyud): btags la dbul//de nas bsreg rdzas dbul bar bya//de dbul ba yi man ngag kyang //khro bo bdag gi cho 'phrul gyis//gzugs brnyan bkug stim btubs te dbul//yang na rdzas mchog mnyes 
17b8(rgyud): ba las//'od zer khro bos bkug cing bsreg//man ngag de la nan tan bya//oM badzra kro d+ha ag na ye che ge mo mA ra ma phaT//stong ma brgya 'am nyi shu rtsa gcig go/yang na bsod nams rim pa yis//
18a1(rgyud): @#//brgya dang stong dang khri ru bya//de ltar byas na las kun 'grub//tha mar dgang blugs bstod bya'o//de yi thugs kar gos sngon bskud//ye shes sems dpa' spyan drangs bstim//
18a2(rgyud): byas btab mchod pa gong bzhin dbul//de nas bsregs sngags gong bzhin dbul//oM badzra kro d+ha nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni rA dz+nya pa ya ti che ge mo mA ra ya hUM phaT//gang na man ngag shes 
18a3(rgyud): par bya//dgang blugs thul la bstod par bya//lcang lo can gyi pho brang nas//gsang ba'i bdag po gos sngon can//sangs rgyas kun gyi sprul pa po//dbang bskur mchog la phyag 'tshal bstod/
18a4(rgyud): 'dag cing gtum la brjid pa'i sku//sna tshogs las rnams mdzad pa po//thogs med yi bzhin nor bu'i tshul//rdo rje sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//dngos po thams cad rnam dag kyang //smon lam 
18a5(rgyud): stobs kyis byung ba'i gsung //tshig dang bral ba'i sna tshogs sgra//rdo rje nor bu khyod la bstod//dpal ldan rdo rje gos sngon can//'khor lo 'bar ba phyag na bsnams//lha rnams thabs kyis 'dul 
18a6(rgyud): bar mdzad//lha'i rgyal po khyod la bstod//dpal dang chen po gos sngon can//bya khyung 'bar ba phyag na bsnams /klu rnams thabs kyis 'dul bar mdzad//klu 'dul gos sngon khyod la 
18a7(rgyud): bstod//dpal ldan chen po gos sngon can/dbyug to 'bar ba phyag na bsnams//gnod sbyin thams cad 'dul mdzad la//gnod sbyin gtso la phyag 'tshal bstod//drag por gyur pa 
18a8(rgyud): 'dul ba'i phyir//sdig 'dzub rtse la 'od byung bas//gdug pa thams cad kun 'dul ba//thogs med rdo rje khyod la bstod//phrin las bcol zhing gshegs su gsol//ting 'dzin bsdus ta 
18b1(rgyud): lta ba gso//rdzas rnams thams cad bsdus nas ni//me'i lha la dbul zhing bstod//yi ge brgya pas lhag chad bkang //ting 'dzin bsdus te spyod lam bya//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje ma 
18b2(rgyud): lce'i rgyud las sbyin bsregs gi cho ga'i le'u ste dgu pa'o////lag na rdo rje gos sngon gyis//bcom ldan mgon la 'di skad gsol//gang dag klu'i zin pa la//thabs de gang lags gsung du 
18b3(rgyud): gsol//kun mkhyen gtso bos bka' stsal pa//nga yi sbyor ba 'di nyid la//brten nas klu rnams gdul bar bya//nub phyogs tha drang po'i dag la//zabs su mi gang brkos byas la//de stengs sngar 
18b4(rgyud): ni bu mo gang //ston mo bcu gnyis byas pa yi//rigs ldan kha dog sngon po'am//rigs bzhi yang dag ldan pa yis//'o mas khrus byas thig le byug/gos dag dmar po bskon byas la//cho ga rab 'bring 
18b5(rgyud): tha ma yi//dbang sdud kha la me 'bar ba'i//bu mo la ni yongs su bsngags//rgyan cha lnga yis rgyan btags la//rkang pa brgyud mar long btags la//lag pa brgyud mar dbang bsgyur du//yang dag gnas pas 
18b6(rgyud): phyag rgya bcas//klu yi ming mtshan gang yin pa'i//dngos po gang la rab sbyar nas//gtso bo kun 'byung mchog nyid las//'khor rnams brgyad kyis nges par bskor//nub du dngos po rten byed pa//gru bzhi pa 
18b7(rgyud): dag rab tu gdags//de nyid sa ru bdag thob ste//de steng ci nus sbyor ba bsgrub//de thob rab tu bral ba las//de yi phyogs su mi lta zhing //drag po chen po'i sngags bzlas bya//de nyid bdag gis mthong ba las//
18b8(rgyud): legs so snyam pa'i tshigs bcad brjod//de nas sa phyogs rab dben par//bdag gis sbyor ba legs pa tsam bya//rgyang ni khru do nges pa las//'bar du chag gang rnam par bya//rngams dang 'bring shun dri chu sogs//
19a1(rgyud): @#//yongs su sbyar ba byas pa dag/ba yi rnam lnga la sogs pas//yongs su byug par byas pa la//sna tshogs pad+ma bcu drug pa//snying po ze mdab rgyas par bya//de steng tshad chung chu skyes 
19a2(rgyud): nyid//phyogs rnams la ni rnam par dgod//de steng sa gnas pa gang yin pa//lha tshogs gzugs su rnam par dgod//phyi rol 'bar ba'i phreng ba yis//yang dag par ni bskor bar bya//
19a3(rgyud): sngon du sor brgyad gang bskor ni//mdog dmar kun nas zlum par phye//de stengs klu rigs drug po dang //nya dang sbal pa lcod mo dang //sbrul dang cang dang ba chu srin dang //mi dang ru sbal ba la da dang 
19a4(rgyud): byil nor bu rgya 'bud rnams//gtso bo'i cha yis gzungs byas nas//shul du drag par drangs byas la//ma tu lung ga'i phye ma yis//bzhi gang rigs thun dngos po na//rab tu gsal bar bya ba yin//
19a5(rgyud): mtshan mo drag po'i gtor ma la//snang ba bzhi par nges par bya//bzlas pa'i sngags ni rab 'bar bas//drag po'i phreng ba bzungs byas la//gur dag sngon pos phub byas la//ji srid de nyid ma rdzogs par

36b8(rgyud): g.yas dang g.yon du bsam par bya//dbu rgyan la sogs rin chen ldan//dbu skra khams ser gyen du 'bar//nag po sbrul lus klu chen brgyad//sku sman stag gi lags pas dkris//chos gos sngon pos bstod dkris te//

37a2(rgyud): g.yas pa gri gug yab la 'khyud//g.yon pa thod pa bde ba ster//gnyis med g.yon gyi phyogs nas 'khril/de rjes sbyor ba'i sgras bskul te//shar phyogs pad+mar nyi zla la//hUM las khams gsum rnam par 
37a3(rgyud): rgyal//dkar po zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa//g.yas pa rdo rje rtse gsum 'dzin//g.yon pa dril bu brtson te//khro bo'i rgyan ldan bsams nas kyang //'byung po'i nus pa 'joms par byed//lho phyogs nyi ma 
37a4(rgyud): ro stengs su//hUM las byung ba'i legs ldan gnag/g.yas pa gri gug g.yon thod pa//zhabs gnyis brkyang bskum thod 'phreng ldan//dgra bo gsod pa'i las la brtson//nub tu klu 'dul bdud rtsi 'khyil//
37a5(rgyud): ljang gu zhal gcig phyag bzhi khro//g.yas pa rdo rje bya khyung dang //g.yon pa dbyug to phur bu bsnams//sa 'go klu sogs zas su gsol//byang du k+Sha las rnam thos bu//zhal gcig phyag gnyis bum stengs 
37a6(rgyud): su||mdog ser g.yas pa rgyal mtshan bsnams//g.yon pas nor sbyin bzung ba ste//dbul ba sel ba'i don la brtson//sgo mtshams lha yi rang bzhin la//ye shes spyan drangs mchod la gzhug/de ni 
37a7(rgyud): yi ge bzhi sbyor yin//lus ngag sems la yi ge yi//lha yi mtshan ma gdab par bya//skye mched lhar byas ting 'dzin gyi//nub pa mi shigs lhar bltas la//ye shes lnga 'dus yi ge bsam//de yi 'od 
37a8(rgyud): kyis spyan drangs pa'i//rig lnga snyoms 'jug byin brlabs kyis//mi bskyod rdo rje dbang bskur te//gsang ba shes rab ye shes kyis//rgyas gdab rigs la bzhe ba yi//phyag rgya che la rigs med do//
37b1(rgyud): 'di ni gsang chen sku sgrub pa//rim gyis gsal zhing rnam par dag/rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sku thob pa//tshe gcig nyid la 'gyur ba yin//rtag tu gom pa'i sbyor ba yis//sa sogs 'jig rten ma lus kun//

37b3(rgyud): lus 'joms//gnod sbyin ma lus bka' nyan cing //lha srin ma lus bran du 'gyur//'di ni gsang ba dam pa ste//mdzes ma khyod la bshad pa yin//bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje gsang ba mngon par

37b7(rgyud): rigs pa dang //phrin las sna tshogs rig pa dang //'khor gyi las kyi rig pa dang //'byung po dgug dang bsad pa dang //gtor ma gtang ba'i rim pa'o//oM badzra pA Ne hUM phaT swA hA/oM badzra kro d+ha na nI laM ba ra N+Da 
37b8(rgyud): ra badzra pA Ni Da hUM phaT swA hA/oM badzra tsa na ra ma hA ro Sha Na hUM phaT swA hA /ta da la shA b+ho b+ha bAn nA ga a d+hi ba ta le /i dan nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra sha rA+e raM/badzra pA Ni hrIM da yaM/yaHsa A tri kra ma gra/ud sA da naM/b+ha be

38b1(rgyud): tsa pa rak+Sha hUM hUM s+thaM b+h+yo dzA kha khA/tsa ka rak+Sha hUM hUM s+thAM b+h+yo dzA kha khA/mun du rak+Sha hUM hUM s+th+waM b+h+yo dzA kha khA/ka ka rak+Sha sa ma ya bud d+ha ya s+phu ta ra/te sha rak+Sha sa ma ya bud d+ha yas+phu ta ra tsa pa rak+Sha sa ma ya bud d+ha yang+ku ta ra/tsa ka 
38b2(rgyud): rak+Sha sa ma ya bud d+ha yaspu ta ra/mun+Ta du du rak+Sha sa ma ya bud d+ha yastu ta ra/hUM hUM hra la hril/di ma di ma/phob phob/sha ra sha ra/di ri di ri rbad rbad/kha thaM kha thaM/khruM/dze re dza re b+h+yo/ha sa ha sa/te pa te pa 
38b3(rgyud): kri ta ha/kri ta ha/kri khug kri khug/tshal pa tshal pa/rbad ling rbad ling /srog tshig nan rbad/lus dul mA ra ya rbad/raM raM dz+wa la dz+wa la shi shi/rdul bun rbad sod/na ga ma ra ya hUM phaT/dzaHhUM baM hUM/d+ha ma ka gi ti 
38b4(rgyud): ra/d+harma te sha sa mun dza/d+harma te sha ta ka ra/d+harma te sha tsa ka ra/d+harma te sha sa ma ya/bud d+ha ya bu ta ra/te pa te pa kha khA ri/te pa te pa ka tsa ri/he pa te pa mun du ri/te pa te pa bu s+t+waM ta ra/te pa te pa sa dza sa muM 
38b5(rgyud): dza/tsi ka ra mun du ra khe/badzra tsa man ran dzA dzA/te sha raM/mun du nan/badzra dzu ru dzu ru raM/dzu ru dzu ru sa muM dza dzA hUM/badzra g+he ne khe dza/badzra pu r+Ni khe dza/badzra mun d+ha d+he khe dza/kha dza rA dza mun du ra khe/kha dza r+Sha mun du ra sa 
38b6(rgyud): muM khe/pad ma ra Ni kha dza rA dzA mun du rA dzA khe/kha dza rA dza mun du sa mun dza ra khe dzu dzu dza khA hi dza/oM kA ya wA ka tsit+ta i daM ba ling /buny+dza dzi g+h+ra phul+la d+hu pa mA sa se/sa kha na ti khun Ni phaT ga ta/oM a mr-i ta bri ta

39b8(rgyud): phag dang lto 'phye'i steng na bzhugs//gshog pa rig bzhi'i rdo rje ste//mjug ma ral gri rigs bzhir ldan//sder chen pags pa me 'bar bas//rab tu gtum zhing sa bdag dang //klu rigs ma lus 
40a1(rgyud): @#//brlag mdzad pa//'og min gnas su sleb par bsam//de rjes gnas gsum yi ges brgyan//oM AHhUM dkar dmar zhing gnag sprul mgo cha'i sngags bsam pa//oM hUM trAM shrI 
40a2(rgyud): A/spyi bo mgrin pa snying ka dang //lte ba gsang gnas bsam par bya//e baM ma yA dpung ba dang //dbyi'i stengs su bsam par bya//hUM swa hI ma brang dang ni//sogs dprag ltod mo lte lkog bsam/
40a3(rgyud): dzaHhUM baM hoHsgo bzhir bsam//staM dza ya a sprul ba dang //yar sprul bsam gyi mi khyab bsam//de nas gsang ba'i sngags bstan pa//las kyi khyung chen dag po'i sngags//sangs rgyas kun gyi mthu

40b1(rgyud): Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra kI la ya sarba big+h+nAn mung+ga ra kI la ya A ko Da ya hUM phaT/oM badzra tsaN Da ga ru Da kI li kI la ya sarba big+hAn mud ga ra kI la ya A ko Ta ya hUM phaT/gtum chung phur bus btab pa'i sngags/

41a1(rgyud): @#//mthing nag gru gsum me 'od ldan//saM las ri rab steng du hUM//'od zer 'phro 'dus rdo rjer bsam//de las gnod sbyin nag po ni//zhal gsum phyag drug zhabs gsum ldan//g.yas 
41a2(rgyud): pa 'jigs pa stag gi zhal//g.yon pa stobs chen seng ge ste//phyag g.yas dang po rdo rje 'dzin//gnyis pa rtse gsum gsum pa phur//g.yon pa dril chen lcags kyu dang //thod pa khrag gis bkang

41a8(rgyud): bi nA sha ka ra ya phaT/ji srid ma shi ri bar du brab//grub nas rigs med thod par gzhug/tse ti chen po'i 'og tu sba//steng du ri rab rdo rje yis//mnan pa byas pas nges par rlag/yang na gnod 
41b1(rgyud): sbyin chen po ste//dur khrod ras la bris byas la//de yi snying gar sngags 'di bri//e yin da du chud par ni//oM badzra yak+Sha kA la tsaN Da sa ma ya ma nu sma ra sid d+hi hUM//phyag la yak+Sha pa ta li//zhabs la kaM maM 
41b2(rgyud): b+ha ra bri//de nyid gtor ma chen po la//bcug ste sngon po'i dar gyis dgab//de snyed ras sam shing lo la//gnod sbyin srog gi 'khor lo ni//lte ba rtsibs bcu mu khyud bcas//yang brtsibs bcu dang mu 
41b3(rgyud): khyud ldan//me 'od ldan pa bri byas te//sngags kyi 'khrul 'khor bskor nas kyang //las kyi ming dang spel ba bri//'di ni gnod sbyin srog yin te//mi 'da' bka' yi dam tshigs yin//las rnams gang bcol 
41b4(rgyud): sku yis byod//yang na rgyal rigs thod pa la//gnod sbyin bris la cha ba'i mar//chang dang bsres la byug bya ste//nyin re gang 'dod las dang sbyar//me tog kha dog 'thun pas brab//bsgo ba'i phrin las 'grub 
41b5(rgyud): par 'gyur//chu dar sngon po'i gzhi ma la//gser gyi 'khor lo bri bar bya//lte ba rtsibs brgyad mu khyud ldan//phyi ma re'u mig bcu drug par//sa bon sngags dang spel bya te//kun gyi mtha' ru phrin las sngags//
41b6(rgyud): phris pa mgul du btags pa yis//mi mthun phyogs rnams bsrung 'gyur te//bung ba mel bya ba bzhin//khyi sha btags pa'i re lu'i nang //dgra yi gzugs bris sngags dang spel//nyi nub bya rog la byin te//
41b7(rgyud): sngags bzlas phyir zhing lan grangs ni//brgya 'am stong du byas gyur na//gang yin gnas nas bskrad par 'gyur//khyi 'brel spu la srad bu byas//mdud pa brgya rtsa brgyad byas te//sngags re zhing yang gnod 
41b8(rgyud): sbyin re//'bras kyi nang du bskyur ba na//phub ma 'bras bar mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin phyi ma du sbyangs pa na//mi phyugs la sogs 'khrigs pa na//khyim dang 'dra bar 'brel bar 'gyur//khyi nag snying du gnod sbyin 
42a1(rgyud): @#//gyis||snying dang sku dang sngags bcug nas//bcings pa tsam gyis gnod sbyin nyid//lus dang grib mang 'dre bar 'gyur//skad cig tsam yang sgrub pa po//de yis stong par mi byed do//de 
42a2(rgyud): las sogs pa'i las kyi rim//mdzes ma khyod la bshad pa yin//bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje gsang ba mngon par bstan pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las gnod sbyin nag po sgrub pa'i le'u ste dgu 
42a3(rgyud): pa'o////dpa' mo rdo rje bdems gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang gi rnal 'byor bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rdzogs pa'i rim pa ji lta bu lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/mdzes

42a5(rgyud): ste||bskyed la dmigs pa thob nas ni//snying kar ye shes sems dpa' bsam//de yi rang bzhin snying nang du//pad+ma 'dab bzhi'i thigs les brgyan//nyi zla'i bar du rgya sran tshang //de yi nang du

80b4(rgyud): gra hA na/du k+Sha sar+b+ba mA/d+hu na d+hu ni/ma tha ma tha/la ra dam ra da/pA Ta ya pA Ta ya/ma Ta ma Ta/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/gu hU d+ha rma saM g+ha/a nu dz+nyA taM kar+m+ma ku ru shI g+h+raM ha ya grI ba ya phaT/badzra ya phaT/badzrA gA trA ya phaT/

85a1(rgyud): @#//sbyor ba 'di yis ni//myur du 'chi'am skam par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje rnam par snang mdzad chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i ye shes rnam par bsgyings pa'i

220a1(rgyud): @#//pa yis//mgul nas 'byung ba'i rtsen pas rol bar byed//pi wang gling bu ya mtshan sgra snyan gyis//glu blangs dngos grub rgya che thob par byed//ji nyid yid du 'ong ba'i glu dag ni//

220b6(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi bdag po shin tu dgyes pa chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i rdo rje ma lus pa sus pa med pa'i rdo rje'i sku dpe med pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i yi ge chen po'i phyag rgya'o////de bzhin o ni

221a1(rgyud): sbyar bas na//dga' byed myur du dngos grub po//rab tu dgyes pa chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i tshad med pa'i yon tan gyi sgra dpe med pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i don gyis dpal thams

221a3(rgyud): sna tshogs chen mo mdun du 'gro//sna tshogs chen mo 'di yis ni//sna tshogs ye shes grub par 'gyur//bdag nyid rdo rje brgyan pa yi//de ni mtshan ldan kun dang rtsen//de nas chos dbyings gnas

221b6(rgyud): kye ma'o spas pa ngo mtshar che//kye ma'o bde ba yang dag 'byung //kye ma'o kun tu bzang po'i don//kye ma'o rab dgyes yang dag 'grogs//kye ma'o bde ba dag pa che//kye ma'o bde ba bde mchog ste//kye

222b1(rgyud): dgongs pa'i dbang dang ldan pas 'gro ba ya mtshan chen po'i sgo nas 'dul mdzad cing //gsang ba'i thig le 'jig rten gsum po 'dir ni dngos grub gtsor bsten te//'jig rten gsum po spyad par bya ba'i 
222b2(rgyud): chos de bde gshegs don du mdzad//ngo mtshar yon tan ldan pa dam tshig bdo las 'byung //bde mdzod bde spyod dga' la rol pa tshul bzhin mdzod//thams cad dga' ba'i rang nyid thig le'i dkyil du

222b6(rgyud): rang bzhin ye shes thob 'dod phyir//dam tshig chen po gsum po 'di la gnas nas ni//zla gsang thig le'i dbyings su bdag cag dogs med 'gro bar bgyi//dbyangs kyi rgyal po 'di yis ni//shin tu srid pas

222b8(rgyud): sems dpar bcas pa dang /rdo rje dang rin po che dang //pad ma dang las kyi rigs kyi btsun mo de dag thams cad la rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa'i bde ba chen pos mnyes par mdzad de/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad

223a3(rgyud): chen po rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa 'dod pa'i dbang po dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas bde bar cang mi gsung bar gyur to//de nas de dag kun dgyes nas//shin tu dgyes pa nyid

223a5(rgyud): gang gi sems can bde nyid kyi//bde bas dkyil 'khor bsgrub par bgyid//de nas bcom ldan 'das dam tshig 'dod pa nye bar longs spyod pa'i dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge'adzin la snyoms par

223b2(rgyud): gar byas te//rdo rje pad mar gzhug byas nas//sems ni bzhug pas bsgrub byas la//de nas sems de blang byas te//blo ldan de yis btud byas te//grub pa gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor du//yang dag dngos

224a2(rgyud): ba shin tu dgyes pa bde ba thob par byed pa dpe med pa'i rdo rjer byed pa'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje'i thal mo de nyid las//khro mo rgyab ni bskum byas te//sor mo lhag ma snga ma bzhin//phyag rgya 'di ni

225b7(rgyud): pa'o//thal mo de nyid bcings pa yin//bcings pa gyen du bzung byas te//mthe'u chung gnyis ni bsgreng byas te//de bzhin sbom po gnyis kyang bya//phyag rgya 'di ni chu srin zhes//rgyal mtshan ldan

226a2(rgyud): par byed//yang dag byang chub gnas pa yi//sangs rgyas 'di yis mnyes par byed//de nas dgyes te sna tshogs che//sna tshogs ye shes skye ba yi//rang gi dam tshig gsungs pa yi//
226a3(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po sgrub par byed//bla med dngos grub thams cad kyang //'di yis thams cad grub par byed//sbyor dang ldan pas byin brlabs te//snying gar nye bar bsam byas na//

226b4(rgyud): mchod byas pa//sbyor pa gar gyis mchod byas na//thams cad las byed dngos grub po//gang dag dngos grub med pa dang //gang dag bde ba med pa'i rnams//de dag phyag rgya 'dis btab na//

226b6(rgyud): gzhan yang mchog tu rgya che ba//munyam dang mi mnyam chos kyi dbyings//btsun mo dam pa de bzhin gshegs//phyag rgya'i sbyor bas dngos grub po//dpal bde ba chen po rdo rje rab tu dgyes pa'i me 
226b7(rgyud): tog gi phreng ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de nyid 'og gi ngos su dor//steng du cung zad bsgrub par bya//'di yis sangs rgyas thabs cad kyi//pad mchog yang ni tshim byed pa//'dis ni bcings

228a1(rgyud): @#//dpal bsten pa//shin tu bde ba'i dkyil 'khor 'jig rten 'dir byung ste/rang bzhin dag pa'i gzungs ma'i dkyil nas bskyed par mdzod//ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i

228b5(rgyud): mchog yang dag gnas//dbus kyi dga' byed stan la ni//dngul la sogs pas rnam par brgyan//sna tshogs pad ma dar gyis g.yogs//thabs cad sten de dag ces bya//dbus su rdo rje shin tu

228b7(rgyud): dbyangs pa yis//dbu la chu bos mdzes par bya//sna tshogs sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag/sna tshogs ye shes rab tu gnas//chu skyes spyan ni yid tsams rgyas//dga' byed yid tsam g.yon du 'dud//
228b8(rgyud): g.yas pa'i phyag gi rdo rje che//snying gar gsor la shin tu brtson//de la sogs pa'i yon tan ldan//thog ma nyid du rnam par gzhag/slob ma 'am yang na gzhan yang rung //mtshan kun gyis ni rnam

229a5(rgyud): yang dag ldan//lo ni bcu drug lon pa'i mchog/shes rab can gyis mdun du gzhag/g.yas su lha mo gsod byed ma//mtshon cha'i mda' ni 'dren par brtson//do shal rkang gdub dag gis

231a2(rgyud): pa yis//dam tshig bsgoms te bsgal bar bya//de nas dam tshig blud byas te//rdo rje bdud rtsi ro yi mchog/dung chen por ni gzhag par bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi'i chu blud do//kye ma dmyal ba yi ni chu 'di ste//
